720 = {
add_to_array = { wet_id_provinces = 2885 }
add_to_array = { wet_id_provinces = 2887 }
add_to_array = { wet_id_provinces = 2889 }
add_to_variable = { wet_id_count_all = 3 }
add_to_variable = { wet_id_count_forest = 0 }
add_to_variable = { wet_id_count_jungle = 0 }
add_to_variable = { wet_id_count_marsh = 0 }
add_to_variable = { wet_id_count_desert = 2 }

}
every_state = {
set_temp_variable = { wet_id_half = wet_id_count_all }
multiply_temp_variable = { wet_id_half = 0.5 }
clr_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_forest
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_count_all > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { wet_id_count_forest < wet_id_half } }
}
set_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_forest
}
clr_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_jungle
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_count_all > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { wet_id_count_jungle < wet_id_half } }
}
set_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_jungle
}
clr_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_marsh
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_count_all > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { wet_id_count_marsh < wet_id_half } }
}
set_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_marsh
}
clr_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_desert
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_count_all > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { wet_id_count_desert < wet_id_half } }
}
set_state_flag = wet_id_terrain_desert
}

}
set_global_flag = wet_id_map_initialized_v1

}

}

wet_id_clear_active = {
clr_country_flag = wet_id_busy
clr_country_flag = wet_id_cancelled
clear_variable = wet_id_current
clear_variable = wet_id_elapsed
clear_variable = wet_id_remaining
clear_variable = wet_id_choice
clear_variable = wet_id_state
clear_variable = wet_id_country
clear_variable = wet_id_payer
clear_array = wet_id_start_states

}

wet_id_repair_one_state = {
set_temp_variable = { wet_id_repaired = 0 }
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_repaired = 0 } check_variable = { damaged_building_level@infrastructure > 0 }
}
set_temp_variable = { wet_id_damage_before = damaged_building_level@infrastructure }
damage_building = {
type = infrastructure
damage = -1
}
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { damaged_building_level@infrastructure < wet_id_damage_before }
}
set_temp_variable = { wet_id_repaired = 1 }
}

}

check_variable = { wet_id_repaired = 0 } check_variable = { damaged_building_level@industrial_complex > 0 }

value = wet_id_repair_province
break = wet_id_repaired
set_temp_variable = { wet_id_damage_before = damaged_building_level@supply_node }
damage_building = {
type = supply_node
province = var:wet_id_repair_province
damage = -1
}

damage = -1
}

wet_id_resolve_venom = {
if = {
limit = {
NOT = { has_country_flag = wet_id_venom_resolved } NOT = { has_country_flag = wet_id_cancelled } var:wet_id_country = { exists = yes has_war_with = ROOT NOT = { has_idea = wet_id_023_poison } }
}
set_country_flag = wet_id_venom_resolved
random_list = {
70 = {
var:wet_id_country = {
add_timed_idea = {
idea = wet_id_023_poison
days = 30
}

}

}
30 = {
var:wet_id_country = {
add_opinion_modifier = {
target = ROOT
modifier = wet_id_023_exposed
days = 180
}

}

}

}

}

}

wet_id_trade_send = {
if = {
limit = {
has_country_flag = wet_id_busy check_variable = { wet_id_current = 15 } check_variable = { wet_id_trade_stage = 1 } FROM = { wet_id_trade_subject_valid = yes }
}
save_event_target_as = wet_identity_seller
FROM = { save_event_target_as = wet_identity_subject }
set_variable = { wet_id_trade_subject = FROM }
set_variable = { wet_id_trade_stage = 2 }
set_variable = { wet_id_elapsed = 0 }
set_variable = { wet_id_remaining = 14 }
set_country_flag = wet_id_trade_offered
var:wet_id_country = {
set_variable = { wet_id_offer_seller = ROOT }
set_variable = { wet_id_offer_subject = ROOT.wet_id_trade_subject }
set_variable = { wet_id_offer_days = 0 }
set_country_flag = wet_id_offer_pending
country_event = { id = wet_identity.15 }
}

}

}

wet_id_clear_offer = {
clr_country_flag = wet_id_offer_pending
clear_variable = wet_id_offer_seller
clear_variable = wet_id_offer_subject
clear_variable = wet_id_offer_days

}

wet_id_trade_refund = {
if = {
limit = {
has_country_flag = wet_id_trade_escrow
}
clr_country_flag = wet_id_trade_escrow
add_political_power = 25
}
clr_country_flag = wet_id_trade_offered
clr_country_flag = wet_id_015_cooldown
set_country_flag = { flag = wet_id_015_cooldown days = 30 }
wet_id_clear_active = yes
clear_variable = wet_id_trade_stage
clear_variable = wet_id_trade_subject

}

wet_id_decline_offer = {
if = {
limit = {
has_country_flag = wet_id_offer_pending
}
var:wet_id_offer_seller = {
if = {
limit = {
has_country_flag = wet_id_trade_escrow check_variable = { wet_id_country = PREV }
}
wet_id_trade_refund = yes
}

}
wet_id_clear_offer = yes
}

}

wet_id_accept_offer = {
if = {
limit = {
wet_id_offer_acceptance = yes
}
clr_country_flag = wet_id_offer_pending
add_political_power = -75
add_intel = {
target = var:wet_id_offer_subject
civilian_intel = 15
army_intel = 15
}
var:wet_id_offer_seller = {
clr_country_flag = wet_id_trade_escrow
clr_country_flag = wet_id_trade_offered
add_political_power = 75
clr_country_flag = wet_id_015_cooldown
set_variable = { wet_id_trade_stage = 3 }
set_variable = { wet_id_elapsed = 0 }
set_variable = { wet_id_remaining = 90 }
set_country_flag = { flag = wet_id_015_cooldown days = 365 }
random_list = {
25 = {
var:wet_id_trade_subject = {
add_opinion_modifier = {
target = PREV
modifier = wet_id_015_exposed
days = 180
}

}

}
75 = {
log = "Identity intelligence sale remained secret."
}

}

}
wet_id_clear_offer = yes

}
else = {
wet_id_decline_offer = yes
}

}

wet_id_offer_daily = {

limit = {
OR = { check_variable = { wet_id_offer_days > 13 } NOT = { wet_id_offer_live = yes } }
}
wet_id_decline_offer = yes
}

}

wet_id_trade_daily = {

limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_trade_stage = 3 }
}
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_elapsed > 89 }
}
wet_id_clear_active = yes
clear_variable = wet_id_trade_stage
clear_variable = wet_id_trade_subject
}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
OR = { NOT = { wet_id_015_identity = yes } has_capitulated = yes check_variable = { wet_id_elapsed > 13 } NOT = { var:wet_id_country = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } }
}
if = {
limit = {
check_variable = { wet_id_trade_stage = 2 }
}
var:wet_id_country = { wet_id_decline_offer = yes }
}
if = {
limit = {
has_country_flag = wet_id_trade_escrow
}
wet_id_trade_refund = yes
}

}

}
